```
+interface AbortSignalCapable<T> {
+  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
+}
+
+function isAbortSignal(value: unknown): value is AbortSignal {
+  return !!value
+    && typeof value === 'object'
+    && 'aborted' in value
+    && typeof (value as AbortSignal).addEventListener === 'function';
+}
+
+function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
+  if (!signal) return query;
+
+  const candidate = query as T & AbortSignalCapable<T>;
+  if (typeof candidate.abortSignal === 'function') {
+    return candidate.abortSignal(signal);
+  }
+
+  return query;
+}
+
+function throwIfAborted(signal?: AbortSignal): void {
+  if (signal?.aborted) {
+    throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
+      recoverable: true,
+      cause: signal.reason,
+    });
+  }
+}
```

```
+/**
+ * Best-effort mark a task as Failed so the UI can update immediately.
+ * Swallows errors — callers still return their own HTTP error response.
+ */
+async function markTaskFailed(
+  supabase: SupabaseClient,
+  taskId: string,
+  errorMessage: string,
+): Promise<void> {
+  try {
+    await supabase.from("tasks").update({
+      status: "Failed",
+      error_message: errorMessage,
+      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+    }).eq("id", taskId).in("status", ["Queued", "In Progress"]);
+  } catch {
+    // Best-effort — don't mask the original error
+  }
+}
+

   task_type: string;

+function defaultErrorCode(status: number): string {
+  if (status === 401) return 'authentication_failed';
+  if (status === 403) return 'forbidden';
+  if (status === 404) return 'not_found';
+  if (status === 405) return 'method_not_allowed';
+  if (status === 429) return 'rate_limited';
+  if (status === 503) return 'service_unavailable';
+  if (status >= 500) return 'internal_server_error';
+  return 'request_failed';
+}
+
+function completeTaskErrorResponse(
+  message: string,
+  status: number,
+  errorCode = defaultErrorCode(status),
+): Response {
+  return edgeErrorResponse(
+    {
+      errorCode,
+      message,
+      recoverable: status >= 500 || status === 429,
+    },
+    status,
+  );
+}
```

---

## Spot-Check Results

### 1. PhilosophyPane.tsx (667 lines changed) -- CLEARLY IMPROVEMENT

This is a **component extraction/decomposition**. The 600+ lines of inline JSX (travel demo, motion reference, LoRA style, closing section) were extracted into named sub-components in a new `sections/PhilosophySections.tsx` file. The parent component now renders:

```tsx
<TravelDemoSection ... />
<MotionReferenceSection ... />
<LoraStyleSection ... />
<PhilosophyClosingSection navigate={navigate} />
```

Also: keyframe CSS moved to a constant, types renamed to be namespaced (`ExampleStyle` -> `PhilosophyExampleStyle`), self-closing JSX tags where appropriate. The new sub-file exists at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/pages/Home/components/panes/sections/PhilosophySections.tsx`.

**Verdict: Clearly an improvement.** Giant component (>300 LOC anti-pattern) broken into focused sections.

---

### 2. File Deletions -- ALL VERIFIED SAFE

**ConstellationCanvas.tsx** (370 lines deleted from `src/shared/components/`):
- **Not dead code -- moved.** The component was relocated to `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/pages/Home/components/hero/ConstellationCanvas.tsx` (the new file is in the untracked list). The only consumer, `HomeBackground.tsx`, has its import updated from `@/shared/components/ConstellationCanvas` to `./ConstellationCanvas`. The old test was also deleted, and a new test exists at the hero location. Zero remaining references to the old path.

**Verdict: Safe move, collocates component with its sole consumer.**

**tourRuntime.ts** (163 lines deleted from `src/shared/components/ProductTour/`):
- Zero imports of `tourRuntime` anywhere in the codebase. The `ProductTour/index.tsx` file was NOT modified, meaning the tour runtime logic was likely already unused or inlined elsewhere. The `ProductTour` component itself and `useProductTour` hook still exist and are still imported by `Layout.tsx` and `useOnboardingFlow.ts`.

**Verdict: Dead code removal. Safe.**

**pairConfig.ts** (183 lines deleted from `generateVideo/`):
- The three exported functions (`extractPairOverrides`, `filterImageShotGenerations`, `buildImagePayload`) were **consolidated into** `generateVideoService.ts` in the same directory. All tests now import from `generateVideoService`. Zero imports reference the deleted `generateVideo/pairConfig` path.

**Verdict: Safe consolidation -- functions moved to the main service file.**

---

### 3. useToolSettings.ts (176 lines) -- MIXED: CLEANUP + BEHAVIORAL IMPROVEMENT

Two categories of change:

**a) Architecture cleanup (neutral/improvement):**
- Direct `supabase()` calls replaced with repository imports (`toolSettingsWriteRepository`). Switch statements for table names replaced with `resolveSettingsScopeTable()`. RPC call replaced with `callUpdateToolSettingsAtomicRpc()`. This aligns with the new eslint rule (see #5) enforcing repository usage.
- `fetchToolSettingsSupabase` + error-check boilerplate replaced with `fetchToolSettingsSupabaseOrThrow`.
- Removed `useEffect` + `updateControllersRef` (AbortController tracking per-mutation). The comment explains: "We intentionally do NOT abort active update mutations on unmount. Settings saves should complete even if the component unmounts." Since abort was never actually triggered, removing the tracking is dead code removal.
- `signal` param removed from mutation function signature since it was only threaded through for cancellation that never happened.

**b) Behavioral addition (improvement):**
- Added `AbortSignal` threading into the `fetchSettingsForScope` and `rawUpdateToolSettings` paths with `throwIfAborted` and `maybeAttachAbortSignal`. This adds cancellation support at the queue level (where it matters) rather than per-component (where it was dead).
- Function overloads added for `updateToolSettingsSupabase` to accept either `(params, mode)` or `(params, signal, mode)`.

**Verdict: Improvement.** Removes dead per-component abort tracking, adds proper cancellation at the write-queue level, and replaces direct Supabase calls with repository pattern. No behavior regression -- the abort controllers were never actually used to cancel anything before.

---

### 4. complete_task/index.ts (94 lines) -- BEHAVIORAL IMPROVEMENT (not just cleanup)

This is **not just cleanup** -- it adds new behavior:

**a) `markTaskFailed` helper (new):** Best-effort marks tasks as "Failed" when errors occur during completion. Previously, on generation creation failure, DB update failure, or uncaught exceptions, the task would be left in "In Progress" limbo. Now the UI can update immediately.

**b) Structured error responses:** `jsonResponse({ error: "..." }, status)` replaced with `completeTaskErrorResponse(message, status, errorCode)` which uses `edgeErrorResponse` (the shared edge function error format). This adds:
- Machine-readable `errorCode` fields (e.g., `storage_path_access_denied`, `task_not_found`, `rate_limit_service_unavailable`)
- `recoverable` flag (true for 5xx and 429)
- Consistent error response shape across all error paths

**c) Auth check added:** New explicit `if (!auth || (!auth.userId && !auth.isServiceRole))` guard returning 401, which was previously implicit/missing.

**Verdict: Improvement, but this is more than cleanup.** The `markTaskFailed` behavior and structured error codes are new features. This should be called out in PR review as a behavioral change -- it will cause tasks to show "Failed" status in the UI where they previously showed stuck "In Progress". That is almost certainly the desired behavior, but it is a change that callers/UI should be aware of.

---

### 5. eslint.config.js -- CLEARLY IMPROVEMENT

Adds a new lint rule restricting direct `@/integrations/supabase/client` imports in three specific high-churn files:
- `src/shared/realtime/RealtimeConnection.ts`
- `src/shared/hooks/useToolSettings.ts`
- `src/domains/generation/hooks/useGenerationMutations.ts`

The error message directs developers to use `@/integrations/supabase/repositories/*` instead.

**Verdict: Improvement.** Enforces the repository pattern that the other changes in this diff introduce. Prevents drift back to direct client usage in these files.

---

### 6. RealtimeConnection.ts (143 lines) -- BEHAVIORAL IMPROVEMENT (race condition fix)

This is a **significant concurrency fix**, not just cleanup:

**a) Connect sequence tracking:** Added `activeConnectSequence` counter, `connectInFlight` promise, and `connectInFlightProjectId`. Every connect attempt gets a sequence number. All async callbacks (`doConnect`, `handleSubscribeFailure`, `scheduleReconnect`, subscribe callback) check `isCurrentConnectAttempt(connectSequence)` before proceeding.

**b) De-duplication:** `startConnect()` returns the existing in-flight promise if a connect is already in progress for the same project, preventing duplicate channel subscriptions.

**c) Stale callback protection:** When a subscribe callback fires for a stale sequence, it now cleans up the channel (`channel.unsubscribe()`) instead of proceeding. The `settled` flag prevents double-resolution of the subscribe promise.

**d) Channel passed explicitly:** `setupEventHandlers` now takes a `channel` parameter instead of using `this.channel`, eliminating a window where `this.channel` could be stale.

**e) `requestRealtimeReconnect` removed:** The import and call to `requestRealtimeReconnect` in `handleSubscribeFailure` was removed. The function still exists in its own file (`src/shared/realtime/requestRealtimeReconnect.ts`) but is no longer called from `RealtimeConnection`. This simplifies the reconnect flow.

**Verdict: Improvement, but this is a significant behavioral change.** This fixes real race conditions (concurrent connects, stale subscribe callbacks), but it also changes reconnection behavior by removing `requestRealtimeReconnect`. If anything else relied on the reconnect request being emitted from `RealtimeConnection`, that coupling is now broken. However, the file still exists -- it just is not called from this path anymore.

---

## Summary

| Area | Verdict | Notes |
|------|---------|-------|
| PhilosophyPane extraction | **Improvement** | Component decomposition, no behavior change |
| ConstellationCanvas deletion | **Improvement** | Moved to colocated location, not deleted |
| tourRuntime deletion | **Improvement** | Dead code, zero imports |
| pairConfig.ts deletion | **Improvement** | Consolidated into generateVideoService.ts |
| useToolSettings.ts | **Improvement** | Repository pattern + proper cancellation |
| complete_task/index.ts | **Improvement, with caveat** | Adds `markTaskFailed` + structured errors = new behavior |
| eslint.config.js | **Improvement** | Enforces repository pattern |
| RealtimeConnection.ts | **Improvement, with caveat** | Fixes race conditions but changes reconnect behavior |

**Overall assessment:** All changes are improvements. Two areas (`complete_task` and `RealtimeConnection`) go beyond cleanup into behavioral improvements that should be specifically noted in PR review. The `complete_task` `markTaskFailed` change means tasks will now transition to "Failed" status on server errors instead of remaining stuck in "In Progress" -- this is almost certainly desired but is a user-visible change. The `RealtimeConnection` race condition fix is substantive enough that it warrants testing the reconnect flow in staging.

```
+/**
+ * Best-effort mark a task as Failed so the UI can update immediately.
+ * Swallows errors — callers still return their own HTTP error response.
+ */
+async function markTaskFailed(
+  supabase: SupabaseClient,
+  taskId: string,
+  errorMessage: string,
+): Promise<void> {
+  try {
+    await supabase.from("tasks").update({
+      status: "Failed",
+      error_message: errorMessage,
+      updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+    }).eq("id", taskId).in("status", ["Queued", "In Progress"]);
+  } catch {
+    // Best-effort — don't mask the original error
+  }
+}

+function defaultErrorCode(status: number): string {
+  if (status === 401) return 'authentication_failed';
+  if (status === 403) return 'forbidden';
+  if (status === 404) return 'not_found';
+  if (status === 405) return 'method_not_allowed';
+  if (status === 429) return 'rate_limited';
+  if (status === 503) return 'service_unavailable';
+  if (status >= 500) return 'internal_server_error';
+  return 'request_failed';
+}
+
+function completeTaskErrorResponse(
+  message: string,
+  status: number,
+  errorCode = defaultErrorCode(status),
+): Response {
+  return edgeErrorResponse(
+    {
+      errorCode,
+      message,
+      recoverable: status >= 500 || status === 429,
+    },
+    status,
+  );
+}
```

```
+interface AbortSignalCapable<T> {
+  abortSignal?: (signal: AbortSignal) => T;
+}
+
+function isAbortSignal(value: unknown): value is AbortSignal {
+  return !!value
+    && typeof value === 'object'
+    && 'aborted' in value
+    && typeof (value as AbortSignal).addEventListener === 'function';
+}
+
+function maybeAttachAbortSignal<T>(query: T, signal?: AbortSignal): T {
+  if (!signal) return query;
+
+  const candidate = query as T & AbortSignalCapable<T>;
+  if (typeof candidate.abortSignal === 'function') {
+    return candidate.abortSignal(signal);
+  }
+
+  return query;
+}
+
+function throwIfAborted(signal?: AbortSignal): void {
+  if (signal?.aborted) {
+    throw new ToolSettingsError('cancelled', 'Request was cancelled', {
+      recoverable: true,
+      cause: signal.reason,
+    });
+  }
+}
```

```
+
+function isCachedVariant(value: unknown): value is CachedVariant {
+  return !!value && typeof value === 'object';

+function patchCachedVariants(
+  queryClient: QueryClient,
+  updateVariant: (variant: CachedVariant) => CachedVariant

+        if (!isCachedVariant(entry)) return entry;
+        const updated = updateVariant(entry);
+        if (updated !== entry) {
+          didPatchThisQuery = true;
+        }
+        return updated;
+      });
+
+      if (!didPatchThisQuery) {
+        return oldData;
+      }
+
+      didPatchAtLeastOneQuery = true;
+      return nextData;
+    }
+  );
+

+    }
+
+    return {

+      viewed_at: viewedAt,
+    };
+  });
```

```
index b6201858..7c8e88cf 100644
--- a/src/shared/lib/clipboard.ts
+++ b/src/shared/lib/clipboard.ts
@@ -2,15 +2,41 @@
  * Best-effort clipboard write that never throws.
  * Returns true when the write succeeds.
  */
-export async function writeClipboardTextSafe(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
-  if (!text || !navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
+export async function writeClipboardTextSafe(
+  text: string,
+  options: { allowExecCommandFallback?: boolean } = {},
+): Promise<boolean> {
+  if (!text) {
     return false;
   }
 
+  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
+    try {
+      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
+      return true;
+    } catch {
+      // fall through to optional fallback
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (!options.allowExecCommandFallback || typeof document === 'undefined') {
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  let textarea: HTMLTextAreaElement | null = null;
   try {
-    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
-    return true;
+    textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
+    textarea.value = text;
+    textarea.style.position = 'fixed';
+    textarea.style.left = '-9999px';
+    document.body.appendChild(textarea);
+    textarea.select();
+    return document.execCommand('copy') === true;
   } catch {
     return false;
+  } finally {
+    if (textarea?.parentNode) {
+      textarea.parentNode.removeChild(textarea);
+    }
   }
 }
```

```
@@ -46,6 +47,33 @@ type EdgeBootstrapResult<TBody extends Record<string, unknown>> =
   | { ok: true; value: EdgeRequestContext<TBody> }
   | { ok: false; response: Response };
 
+function deriveErrorCode(message: string, status: number): string {
+  const normalized = message.trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (normalized === 'method not allowed' || status === 405) {
+    return 'method_not_allowed';
+  }
+  if (normalized === 'authentication failed' || status === 401) {
+    return 'authentication_failed';
+  }
+  if (
+    normalized === 'unauthorized - service role required'
+    || (normalized.includes('unauthorized') && normalized.includes('service role'))
+  ) {
+    return 'service_role_required';
+  }
+  if (normalized === 'internal server error' || status >= 500) {
+    return 'internal_server_error';
+  }
+  if (status === 403) {
+    return 'forbidden';
+  }
+  return 'request_failed';
+}
+
+function isRecoverableStatus(status: number): boolean {
+  return status >= 500 || status === 429;
+}
+
 function buildErrorResponse(
   message: string,
   status: number,
@@ -58,21 +86,15 @@ function buildErrorResponse(
       headers: extraHeaders,
     });
   }
-
-  const response = jsonResponse({ error: message }, status);
-  if (!extraHeaders || Object.keys(extraHeaders).length === 0) {
-    return response;
-  }
-
-  const headers = new Headers(response.headers);
-  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(extraHeaders)) {
-    headers.set(key, value);
-  }
-
-  return new Response(response.body, {
-    status: response.status,
-    headers,
-  });
+  return edgeErrorResponse(
+    {
+      errorCode: deriveErrorCode(message, status),
+      message,
+      recoverable: isRecoverableStatus(status),
+    },
+    status,
+    extraHeaders,
+  );
 }
```

```
+function resolveVideoUploadOptions(

+  onProgress?: (progress: number) => void,

+  if (maxRetriesOrOptions && typeof maxRetriesOrOptions === 'object') {
+    return {
+      onProgress: maxRetriesOrOptions.onProgress,
+      maxRetries: maxRetriesOrOptions.maxRetries ?? 3,
+      signal: maxRetriesOrOptions.signal,
+      timeoutMs: maxRetriesOrOptions.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_VIDEO_TIMEOUT_MS,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return {
+    onProgress,
+    maxRetries: typeof maxRetriesOrOptions === 'number' ? maxRetriesOrOptions : 3,
+    signal: undefined,
+    timeoutMs: DEFAULT_VIDEO_TIMEOUT_MS,
+  };
```

```
diff --git a/src/shared/lib/promptAssembly.ts b/src/shared/lib/promptAssembly.ts
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..2b6ee873
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/lib/promptAssembly.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,26 @@
+export type PromptAssemblyPolicy = 'batch_comma' | 'legacy_batch_comma' | 'segment_space';
+
+const POLICY_DELIMITER: Record<PromptAssemblyPolicy, string> = {
+  batch_comma: ', ',
+  legacy_batch_comma: ', ',
+  segment_space: ' ',
+};
+
+function normalizePart(part: string | null | undefined): string {
+  return (part ?? '').trim();
+}
+
+/**
+ * Compose prompt fragments with an explicit normalization + delimiter policy.
+ * Keeping the policy named at call sites prevents hidden drift between paths.
+ */
+export function joinPromptParts(
+  parts: Array<string | null | undefined>,
+  policy: PromptAssemblyPolicy,
+): string {
+  const delimiter = POLICY_DELIMITER[policy];
+  return parts
+    .map(normalizePart)
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .join(delimiter);
+}
```

```
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..37a74c3b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/shared/contexts/contextGuard.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+/**
+ * Enforces a consistent provider-guard error for context hooks.
+ */
+export function requireContextValue<T>(
+  context: T | undefined | null,
+  hookName: string,
+  providerName: string,
+): T {
+  if (context === undefined || context === null) {
+    const article = /^a/i.test(providerName) ? 'an' : 'a';
+    throw new Error(
+      `${hookName} must be used within ${article} ${providerName}. ` +
+        `Make sure the component is rendered inside the ${providerName} tree.`,
+    );
+  }
+  return context;
+}
```

```
+  const trimmed = value.trim();
+  return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
+}
+
+export function getPendingJoinClipsStorageKey(
+  projectId: string | null | undefined,
+  userId?: string | null,
+): string {
+  const normalizedProjectId = normalizeScopePart(projectId);
+  if (!normalizedProjectId) {
+    return LEGACY_PENDING_JOIN_CLIPS_KEY;
+  }
+  const normalizedUserId = normalizeScopePart(userId) ?? ANONYMOUS_SCOPE;
+  return `${PENDING_JOIN_CLIPS_KEY_PREFIX}:${normalizedProjectId}:${normalizedUserId}`;
+}
+
+export function getPendingJoinClipsCandidateKeys(
+  scope: PendingJoinClipScope,
+): string[] {
+  const [REDACTED](scope.projectId, scope.userId);
+  return scopedKey === LEGACY_PENDING_JOIN_CLIPS_KEY
+    ? [LEGACY_PENDING_JOIN_CLIPS_KEY]
+    : [scopedKey, LEGACY_PENDING_JOIN_CLIPS_KEY];
+}
+
+export function isPendingJoinClipInScope(
+  entry: PendingJoinClipEntry,
+  scope: PendingJoinClipScope,
+): boolean {
+  const normalizedProjectId = normalizeScopePart(scope.projectId);
+  if (normalizedProjectId) {
+    const entryProjectId = normalizeScopePart(entry.projectId);
+    if (entryProjectId && entryProjectId !== normalizedProjectId) {
+      return false;
+    }
+  }
+
+  const normalizedUserId = normalizeScopePart(scope.userId);
+  if (normalizedUserId) {
+    const entryUserId = normalizeScopePart(entry.userId);
+    if (entryUserId && entryUserId !== normalizedUserId) {
+      return false;
+    }
+  }
+
+  return true;
+}
```

```
+import type { AppError } from './errors';

+export type RuntimeErrorPresenter = (appError: AppError, toastTitle?: string) => boolean;
+
+let presenter: RuntimeErrorPresenter | null = null;
+
+export function installRuntimeErrorPresenter(nextPresenter: RuntimeErrorPresenter): void {
+  presenter = nextPresenter;
+}
+
+export function presentRuntimeError(appError: AppError, toastTitle?: string): boolean {
+  if (!presenter) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  return presenter(appError, toastTitle);
+}
```

```
+  realtime,

+  reportCorruption,
+  recordEvent,
+  getTimelineSnapshot,

+  if (realtime.__REFERENCE_TRACKING_INSTALLED__) {
+    return;
+  }
+
+  let trackedSocket = realtime.socket;
+  Object.defineProperty(realtime, 'socket', {

+          corruptionTimeline: getTimelineSnapshot(),
+          timestamp: Date.now(),
+        });

+          previousValue: trackedSocket,
+          newValue: value,
+        });
+      } else if (!trackedSocket && value) {
+        recordEvent('SOCKET_SET_TO_WEBSOCKET', { newValue: value });

+        reportCorruption('[RealtimeCorruptionTrace] realtime.socket replaced', {
```

```
+export const extractSegmentImages = (

+    if (typeof url !== 'string') return undefined;
+    return url.replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');

+

+  const explicitStartUrl = cleanUrl(
+    asString(individualSegmentParams.start_image_url) ?? asString(taskParams.start_image_url),
+  );
+  const explicitEndUrl = cleanUrl(

+  );
+  const explicitStartGenId = asString(individualSegmentParams.start_image_generation_id)
+    ?? asString(taskParams.start_image_generation_id);
+  const explicitEndGenId = asString(individualSegmentParams.end_image_generation_id)
+    ?? asString(taskParams.end_image_generation_id);
+
+  const allUrls = asStringArray(orchestratorDetails.input_image_paths_resolved)
+    ?? asStringArray(taskParams.input_image_paths_resolved)
+    ?? [];
+  const allGenIds = asStringArray(orchestratorDetails.input_image_generation_ids)
+    ?? asStringArray(taskParams.input_image_generation_ids)
+    ?? [];
+
+  const arrayStartUrl = cleanUrl(allUrls[segmentIndex]);
+  const arrayEndUrl = cleanUrl(allUrls[segmentIndex + 1]);
+  const arrayStartGenId = allGenIds[segmentIndex];
```

```
+/**
+ * Hook to hydrate reference pointers with full data from resources table
+ * Converts lightweight ReferenceImage pointers to full HydratedReferenceImage objects
+ * Searches both user's own resources and public resources
+ */
+export const useHydratedReferences = (
+  referencePointers: ReferenceImage[] | undefined
+): {
+  hydratedReferences: HydratedReferenceImage[];
+  isLoading: boolean;
+  hasLegacyReferences: boolean;
+} => {
+  // Get current user ID from context for ownership check (avoids extra auth call)
+  const { userId: currentUserId } = useProjectIdentityContext();
+
+  // Extract resource IDs we need to fetch
+  const resourceIdsToFetch = useMemo(() => {
+    if (!referencePointers) return [];
+    return referencePointers
+      .map(p => p.resourceId)
+      .filter(id => !!id); // Only valid IDs
+  }, [referencePointers]);
+
+  // Use optimized fetch for specific resources
+  const specificResources = useSpecificResources(resourceIdsToFetch);
```

# Branch Summary: `chore/desloppify-queue-2026-03-02`

**298 files changed, +7254 / -4626 (net +2628, or net -2628 excluding desloppify artifacts)**

This is a large cleanup/quality branch. The major themes are:
1. Dead code removal (43 files deleted)
2. Extraction of inline Supabase calls into repository modules
3. Extraction of large inline functions into standalone modules
4. Hardening edge functions with structured error responses and explicit auth checks
5. Race condition fixes (realtime connection, join clips hydration)
6. New tests for previously untested code
7. Deduplication of masked-edit task builders (inpaint + annotated edit)

---

## Modified Files: Key Changes

### 1. `src/shared/realtime/RealtimeConnection.ts`
Added **connection deduplication** via `activeConnectSequence` / `connectInFlight` tracking. `startConnect()` now deduplicates concurrent connect calls for the same projectId using a sequence counter pattern. Each connect attempt checks `isCurrentConnectAttempt()` before mutating state. Also extracted direct `supabase()` calls into repository imports (`fetchRealtimeSession`, `setRealtimeAuthToken`, `createRealtimeChannel`).

### 2. `supabase/functions/complete_task/index.ts`
Three major additions: (a) `markTaskFailed()` best-effort helper to update task status on errors; (b) `completeTaskErrorResponse()` with structured error codes via `edgeErrorResponse`; (c) explicit auth guard (`if (!auth || (!auth.userId && !auth.isServiceRole))`). All error paths now return structured JSON with `errorCode`, `message`, `recoverable` fields instead of plain text.

### 3. `src/shared/hooks/useToolSettings.ts`
Extracted inline Supabase calls to `toolSettingsWriteRepository`. Added `AbortSignal` support (`maybeAttachAbortSignal`, `throwIfAborted`). Added `SettingsWriteMode` type. Removed `useEffect` import (no longer needed). The `fetchSettingsForScope` function now accepts and threads an abort signal.

### 4. `src/shared/hooks/useMarkVariantViewed.ts`
Major refactor extracting **optimistic cache update helpers** into standalone functions: `patchCachedVariants()`, `markVariantViewedInCache()`, `markGenerationViewedInCache()`, `decrementBadgeCountOptimistically()`. These were previously inline anonymous functions. The logic is the same but now testable and reusable.

### 5. `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.tsx`
Split `useProject()` into `useProjectSelectionContext()` + `useProjectCrudContext()`. Added `LightboxSessionModel` type and a `lightboxSession` memo that organizes all lightbox props into structured sections (core, navigation, actions, shotWorkflow, ui, optimistic, taskModal, taskData). Added `resolveLightboxDeletingId()` helper.

### 6. `src/tools/join-clips/hooks/useClipManager.ts`
Fixed **race conditions** during project switching. Added `initialHydrationComplete`, `activeProjectIdRef`, `hydrationRequestVersionRef`, `pendingConsumeVersionRef` to track staleness. The `consumePendingJoinClips` effect now accepts a `projectId` param, checks version numbers before applying state, and uses a cleanup function to cancel stale requests.

### 7. `src/shared/lib/tasks/annotatedImageEdit.ts`
Replaced ~100 lines of inline task creation with a 2-line delegation to new `createMaskedEditTask()` from `maskedEditTaskBuilder.ts`. The type is now re-exported as an alias.

### 8. `src/shared/lib/clipboard.ts`
Added `execCommand('copy')` fallback when `navigator.clipboard` is unavailable. New `options.allowExecCommandFallback` parameter. Creates a hidden textarea, selects, and copies. Previously would just return false on clipboard API failure.

### 9. `src/integrations/supabase/instrumentation/window/index.ts`
Extracted ~150 lines of inline WebSocket patching / event collection into `eventCollectors.ts` and `globalPatchers.ts`. The index file now just orchestrates the extracted modules.

### 10. `src/integrations/supabase/instrumentation/realtime/index.ts`
Extracted ~80 lines of inline `Object.defineProperty` socket tracking into `referencePatchers.ts` and diagnostic reporting into `diagnosticReporters.ts`. The index file is now ~30 lines of orchestration.

### 11. `src/domains/generation/hooks/useGenerationMutations.ts`
Replaced 6 inline `supabase().from('generations')` / `supabase().from('generation_variants')` calls with imports from `generationMutationsRepository.ts`: `createGenerationRecord`, `createGenerationPrimaryVariant`, `updateGenerationLocationByScope`, `updateGenerationStarByScope`, `deleteGenerationByScope`, `deleteGenerationVariantByScope`.

### 12. `supabase/functions/_shared/edgeHandler.ts`
Replaced `buildErrorResponse` to use `edgeErrorResponse()` with structured fields (`errorCode`, `message`, `recoverable`). Added `deriveErrorCode()` and `isRecoverableStatus()` helpers. Error responses are now consistent JSON instead of mixed text/JSON.

### 13. `supabase/functions/calculate-task-cost/index.ts`
Eliminated a **second DB query** for `task_types` by joining via FK in the initial task query (`task_types!tasks_task_type_fkey(...)`). Added `TaskTypeConfig` interface. The separate `.from('task_types').select('*')` call is replaced by `typedTask.task_type_config`.

### 14. `src/shared/lib/videoUploader.ts`
Refactored `uploadVideoToStorage` signature: removed unused `_projectId` and `_shotId` params. Added `resolveVideoUploadOptions()` to support both old `(file, maxRetries, onProgress)` and new `(file, options)` signatures cleanly. Exported `VideoUploadOptions` interface.

### 15. `src/shared/lib/tasks/imageInpaint.ts`
Same as annotatedImageEdit: replaced ~90 lines with 2-line delegation to `createMaskedEditTask()`. Combined with #7, this eliminates ~190 lines of duplicated task creation code.

---

## Other Notable Areas

**Contexts** (`src/shared/contexts/`): New `contextGuard.ts` provides a generic `requireContextValue()` used across `ProjectContext`, `AuthContext`, `CurrentShotContext`, `AIInputModeContext`, and `UserSettingsContext` to replace bespoke missing-provider errors. `ToolSettingsServiceContext.tsx` deleted (dead code).

**MediaLightbox** (24 files, +1073/-459): Major extraction of StrokeOverlay into `strokeOverlay/` subdirectory (canvas + mask export), deletion of `MediaLightboxInputAdapter` and `AnnotationActionOverlay`, expanded tests for variant badges and variant selection, refactored `useSharedLightboxState` and `useVideoLightboxRenderModel`.

**Supabase integrations** (16 files, +715/-362): Three new repository files extract inline DB calls. Instrumentation modules split from monolithic files into focused modules. `legacySupabaseAllowlist.json` deleted.

**realtime/index.ts**: Trimmed block comment documentation (the barrel file doesn't need an essay).

## What to look for
For each file, assess:
- **Correctness**: Does the change preserve behavior? Any logic bugs introduced?
- **Completeness**: If something was extracted/moved, is the wiring complete?
- **Dead code**: Any new dead code introduced?
- **Type safety**: Any `as any` casts, unsafe assertions, or widened types?
- **Missing error handling**: Any new async paths without error handling?
- **Race conditions**: Any new async state updates that could race?

## Output format
Group findings by severity:
1. **Problems** — things that look wrong or will break
2. **Concerns** — things that smell off but might be fine with context
3. **Clean** — summary of files that look clearly sound

Be thorough. Read the actual diffs, don't just look at stats.

```
1→import Konva from 'konva';
     2→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     3→import type { BrushStroke } from '../../hooks/inpainting/types';
     4→
     5→interface ExportStrokeMaskInput {
     6→  strokes: BrushStroke[];
     7→  imageWidth: number;
     8→  imageHeight: number;
     9→  pixelRatio?: number;
    10→}
    11→
    12→const DEFAULT_EXPORT_PIXEL_RATIO = 1.5;
    13→const MASK_BACKGROUND = 'black';
    14→const MASK_STROKE = 'white';
    15→const RECTANGLE_MASK_STROKE_WIDTH = 6;
    16→
    17→function addStrokeToMaskLayer(layer: Konva.Layer, stroke: BrushStroke): void {
    18→  if (stroke.points.length < 2) {
    19→    return;
    20→  }
    21→
    22→  if (stroke.shapeType === 'rectangle') {
    23→    if (stroke.isFreeForm && stroke.points.length === 4) {
    24→      layer.add(new Konva.Line({
    25→        points: stroke.points.flatMap(point => [point.x, point.y]),
    26→        stroke: MASK_STROKE,
    27→        strokeWidth: RECTANGLE_MASK_STROKE_WIDTH,
    28→        closed: true,
    29→        lineCap: 'round',
    30→        lineJoin: 'round',
    31→      }));
    32→      return;
    33→    }
    34→
    35→    const start = stroke.points[0];
    36→    const end = stroke.points[1];
    37→    layer.add(new Konva.Rect({
    38→      x: Math.min(start.x, end.x),
    39→      y: Math.min(start.y, end.y),
    40→      width: Math.abs(end.x - start.x),
    41→      height: Math.abs(end.y - start.y),
    42→      stroke: MASK_STROKE,
    43→      strokeWidth: RECTANGLE_MASK_STROKE_WIDTH,
    44→    }));
    45→    return;
    46→  }
    47→
    48→  layer.add(new Konva.Line({
    49→    points: stroke.points.flatMap(point => [point.x, point.y]),
    50→    stroke: MASK_STROKE,
    51→    strokeWidth: stroke.brushSize,
    52→    lineCap: 'round',
    53→    lineJoin: 'round',
    54→  }));
    55→}
    56→
    57→export function exportStrokeMask({
    58→  strokes,
    59→  imageWidth,
    60→  imageHeight,
    61→  pixelRatio = DEFAULT_EXPORT_PIXEL_RATIO,
    62→}: ExportStrokeMaskInput): string | null {
    63→  if (strokes.length === 0) {
    64→    return null;
    65→  }
    66→
    67→  const container = document.createElement('div');
    68→  container.style.position = 'absolute';
    69→  container.style.left = '-9999px';
    70→  document.body.appendChild(container);
    71→
    72→  try {
    73→    const stage = new Konva.Stage({
    74→      container,
    75→      width: imageWidth,
    76→      height: imageHeight,
    77→    });
    78→    const layer = new Konva.Layer();
    79→    stage.add(layer);
    80→
    81→    layer.add(new Konva.Rect({
    82→      x: 0,
    83→      y: 0,
    84→      width: imageWidth,
    85→      height: imageHeight,
    86→      fill: MASK_BACKGROUND,
    87→    }));
    88→
    89→    strokes.forEach((stroke) => addStrokeToMaskLayer(layer, stroke));
    90→
    91→    layer.draw();
    92→    const dataUrl = stage.toDataURL({
    93→      pixelRatio,
    94→      mimeType: 'image/png',
    95→    });
    96→    stage.destroy();
    97→    return dataUrl;
    98→  } catch (error) {
    99→    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
   100→      context: 'StrokeOverlay.exportMask',
   101→      showToast: false,
   102→    });
   103→    return null;
   104→  } finally {
   105→    document.body.removeChild(container);
   106→  }
   107→}
   108→
```

```
+function toDownloadServiceError(params: {
+  error: unknown;
+  context: string;
+  url?: string;
+  fallbackMessage: string;
+}): DownloadServiceError {
+  if (params.error instanceof DownloadServiceError) {
+    return params.error;
+  }
+
+  if (params.error instanceof DOMException && params.error.name === 'AbortError') {
+    return new DownloadServiceError({
+      code: 'aborted',
+      context: params.context,
+      message: 'Download request was aborted',
+      url: params.url,
+    });
+  }
+
+  return new DownloadServiceError({
+    code: 'network_error',
+    context: params.context,
+    message: params.error instanceof Error && params.error.message
+      ? params.error.message
+      : params.fallbackMessage,
+    url: params.url,
+  });
+}
```

```
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
+import type { GeneratedImageWithMetadata } from '@/shared/components/MediaGallery/types';
+import { downloadBlobAsFile } from '@/shared/runtime/browserDownloadRuntime';
+import {
+  DownloadServiceError,
+  downloadStarredMediaArchive,
+} from '../downloadService';
+
+vi.mock('@/shared/lib/mediaUrl', () => ({
+  getDisplayUrl: (url: string) => url,
+}));
+
+vi.mock('@/shared/runtime/browserDownloadRuntime', () => ({
+  downloadBlobAsFile: vi.fn(),
+}));
+
+const mockZipFile = vi.fn();
+const mockZipGenerateAsync = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Blob(['zip-data'], { type: 'application/zip' }));
+
+vi.mock('jszip', () => ({
+  default: vi.fn().mockImplementation(function MockZip() {
+    return {
+      file: mockZipFile,
+      generateAsync: mockZipGenerateAsync,
+    };
+  }),
+}));
+
+function buildImage(overrides: Partial<GeneratedImageWithMetadata> = {}): GeneratedImageWithMetadata {
+  return {
+    id: 'gen-1',
+    url: 'https://example.com/image.png',
+    isVideo: false,
+    metadata: {},
+    ...overrides,
+  } as GeneratedImageWithMetadata;
+}
+
+describe('downloadStarredMediaArchive', () => {
+  beforeEach(() => {
+    vi.clearAllMocks();
+  });
+
+  afterEach(() => {
+    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
+  });
+
+  it('wraps fetch network failures as DownloadServiceError', async () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('network down')));
+
+    await expect(
+      downloadStarredMediaArchive({ images: [buildImage()] }),
+    ).rejects.toMatchObject<Partial<DownloadServiceError>>({
+      name: 'DownloadServiceError',
+      code: 'network_error',
+      context: 'MediaGallery.downloadStarredMediaArchive',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('wraps abort failures as DownloadServiceError with aborted code', async () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      'fetch',
+      vi.fn().mockRejectedValue(new DOMException('Aborted', 'AbortError')),
+    );
+
+    await expect(
+      downloadStarredMediaArchive({ images: [buildImage()] }),
+    ).rejects.toMatchObject<Partial<DownloadServiceError>>({
+      name: 'DownloadServiceError',
+      code: 'aborted',
+      context: 'MediaGallery.downloadStarredMediaArchive',
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('preserves http failures as DownloadServiceError', async () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      'fetch',
+      vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
+        ok: false,
+        status: 503,
+        statusText: 'Service Unavailable',
+      } satisfies Partial<Response>),
+    );
+
+    await expect(
+      downloadStarredMediaArchive({ images: [buildImage()] }),
+    ).rejects.toMatchObject<Partial<DownloadServiceError>>({
+      name: 'DownloadServiceError',
+      code: 'http_error',
+      status: 503,
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('downloads a zip archive on success', async () => {
+    vi.stubGlobal(
+      'fetch',
+      vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
+        ok: true,
+        status: 200,
+        statusText: 'OK',
+        blob: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Blob(['image-data'], { type: 'image/png' })),
+      } satisfies Partial<Response>),
+    );
+
+    const result = await downloadStarredMediaArchive({
+      images: [buildImage()],
+    });
+
+    expect(result.count).toBe(1);
+    expect(result.archiveFilename.endsWith('.zip')).toBe(true);
+    expect(mockZipFile).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(downloadBlobAsFile).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+  });
+});
```

```
   193→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
   194→export function updateToolSettingsSupabase(
   195→  params: UpdateToolSettingsParams,
   196→  signalOrMode?: AbortSignal | SettingsWriteMode,
   197→  maybeMode: SettingsWriteMode = 'debounced',
   198→): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
   199→  const { scope, id, toolId, patch } = params;

   204→    scope,
   205→    entityId: id,
   206→    toolId,
   207→    patch: patch as Record<string, unknown>,
   208→    ...(signal ? { signal } : {}),
   209→  }, mode) as Promise<Record<string, unknown>>;
```

# MediaLightbox / MediaGallery Audit Results

## 1. PROBLEMS -- things that look wrong or will break

### P1. `showMagicEdit` silently changed from `true` to `false`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/components/MediaGalleryLightbox.tsx`

In the old code, `MediaGalleryLightbox` passed `showMagicEdit: true` through the adapter. The new code passes `showMagicEdit: false` as a flat prop:

```ts
showMagicEdit: false,
```

This is accompanied by the removal of the `onMagicEdit` handler that previously logged a "not wired" error. So the change looks intentional -- the old code was showing a magic edit button that did nothing (just logged an error), and this cleans it up by disabling the feature flag. However, if any downstream code actually wires `onMagicEdit` in the gallery context now, the button will no longer render. **This is likely correct** -- the old code was effectively broken (`onMagicEdit` threw a logged error), and disabling the flag is the honest thing to do. But it is a behavior change worth flagging.

**Verdict:** Probably intentional, but verify that magic edit in the gallery was always a dead feature.

### P2. `isDeleting` type narrowing loses `boolean` input from gallery consumers

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.tsx`

The old code passed `isDeleting` (which is `string | boolean | null`) directly to the lightbox. The new code introduces `resolveLightboxDeletingId()` which converts `boolean` to a string (the active media ID) or null:

```ts
function resolveLightboxDeletingId(
  isDeleting: string | boolean | null | undefined,
  activeMediaId: string | undefined,
): string | null {
  if (typeof isDeleting === 'string') return isDeleting;
  if (isDeleting && activeMediaId) return activeMediaId;
  return null;
}
```

The old `MediaGalleryLightbox` adapter also did a type narrowing: `typeof isDeleting === 'string' || isDeleting == null ? isDeleting : undefined`. That conversion discarded `boolean` values by mapping `true` to `undefined`, which meant the delete button was never disabled when `isDeleting === true`. The new code is actually **better**: when `isDeleting === true`, it resolves to the active media's ID, so the delete button will correctly show a disabled/loading state for the right item. This is a behavior fix, not a regression.

**Verdict:** Sound improvement. No issue.

---

## 2. CONCERNS -- things that smell off but might be fine with context

### C1. `maskExport.test.ts` has only one test (empty strokes)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/strokeOverlay/__tests__/maskExport.test.ts`

The test file only covers the trivial `strokes: []` case. It does not test actual mask export with strokes, rectangles, free-form shapes, or error handling. Given that `exportStrokeMask` creates DOM elements and Konva stages, the meaningful paths are untested. This is minimal coverage for what looks like a non-trivial extraction.

**Verdict:** Test coverage is shallow but the function is extracted from working code, so the risk is low. Worth adding tests later.

### C2. `enhancedMedia` merge order may hide stale active media fields

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/components/MediaGalleryLightbox.tsx`

```ts
const sourceMedia = filteredImages.find((img) => img.id === activeLightboxMedia.id) ?? activeLightboxMedia;
...
return {
  ...activeLightboxMedia,
  ...sourceMedia,
  starred: sourceMedia.starred ?? activeLightboxMedia.starred ?? false,
  metadata: cleanMetadata,
};
```

The spread `...activeLightboxMedia` followed by `...sourceMedia` means `sourceMedia` wins for all overlapping fields, which is correct. However, the old code had a React Query cache subscription (`cacheVersion`) that ensured real-time `starred` updates even if `filteredImages` hadn't re-rendered yet. The new code removes that subscription entirely. If the gallery is not re-rendering `filteredImages` quickly enough after a star toggle, the lightbox could show a stale `starred` value until the next render cycle.

The comment says "no cache side-channel subscriptions" which suggests this was an intentional simplification -- relying on React Query invalidation to update `filteredImages` rather than subscribing to the query cache directly.

**Verdict:** Likely correct if React Query invalidation is working properly for star mutations. The old approach was a workaround for a re-render timing issue. If starring seems slow to reflect in the lightbox, this is why.

### C3. Duplicate `handleDelete` async wrapper in three locations

The same `handleDelete` async pattern appears in:
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useSharedLightboxState.ts` (line ~580)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useVideoLightboxActions.ts` (line ~42)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/ImageLightbox.tsx` (line ~732)

All three do:
```ts
const handleDelete = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
  if (!onDelete) return;
  try {
    await Promise.resolve(onDelete(media.id));
  } catch (error) {
    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
      context: 'XxxLightbox.delete',
      toastTitle: 'Delete Failed',
    });
  }
}, [onDelete, media.id]);
```

This is three identical implementations of the same error-handling wrapper. It works, but it's a maintenance concern -- if the error handling pattern changes, three places need to be updated.

**Verdict:** Working but duplicated. Not a bug, just a code quality note.

### C4. `submitSegmentTask` now throws on `saveSettings` returning `false`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/submitSegmentTask.ts`

```ts
async function maybeSaveSettings(runtime: SubmitSegmentRuntime): Promise<void> {
  if (runtime.shouldSaveSettings) {
    const didSave = await runtime.saveSettings();
    if (!didSave) {
      throw new Error('Failed to save segment settings before task submission');
    }
  }
}
```

The old code was `await runtime.saveSettings()` with no return value check (return was `Promise<boolean | void>`). The type was tightened to `Promise<boolean>` and now it throws on `false`. This is a behavior change: previously, if settings save failed silently (returned `false`), the task would still be created. Now it aborts. The test confirms this is intentional.

**Verdict:** This is a correctness improvement -- you shouldn't create a task if settings didn't persist. But it does change the failure behavior from "silently proceed" to "abort with error," which could surface user-facing errors that were previously hidden.

### C5. `useJoinClips` now scopes storage key per project/user

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useJoinClips.ts`

The old code used a fixed `'pendingJoinClips'` key. The new code uses `getPendingJoinClipsStorageKey(selectedProjectId, userId)` which produces scoped keys like `pendingJoinClips:proj-123:user-456`. Existing pending clips stored under the old key will not be found under the new scoped key.

Looking at `joinClipsPendingQueue.ts`, the `getPendingJoinClipsCandidateKeys` function reads from **both** the scoped key and the legacy key, so the consumer (join clips page) should still find old entries. But the write in `useJoinClips` always writes to the scoped key now. This means:
- Old clips are readable (candidate keys include legacy)
- New clips are written to scoped key
- No migration of old entries happens

**Verdict:** This is fine -- old clips remain accessible through the candidate key fallback, and new clips go to the right scoped location. One-way migration is acceptable since pending clips are ephemeral.

### C6. `useVariantSelection` dependency array includes `media` AND `viewedGenerationId`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/hooks/useVariantSelection.ts`

The `setActiveVariantId` callback depends on `[rawSetActiveVariantId, markViewed, media, viewedGenerationId]`. Including `media` as a whole object in the dependency array means the callback is recreated on every `media` reference change, even if only unrelated fields changed. The old code had the same issue (it already included `media`), so this is not a regression -- just an existing smell preserved.

**Verdict:** Not a regression, but `media` in the deps array could cause unnecessary re-renders.

### C7. `downloadService.ts` test uses `as` cast for `Partial<Response>`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/services/__tests__/downloadService.test.ts`

```ts
vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({
  ok: false, status: 503, statusText: 'Service Unavailable',
} satisfies Partial<Response>));
```

Using `satisfies Partial<Response>` is actually safer than `as Response` -- it validates the shape at the type level. This is fine.

**Verdict:** Clean test pattern, no issue.

---

## 3. CLEAN -- files that are clearly sound

### Deleted files (items 1-2)
- **`MediaLightboxInputAdapter.tsx`** -- Deleted. Zero references remain in the codebase. The barrel file `MediaLightbox.tsx` was updated to remove the re-export. All consumers now import `MediaLightbox` directly. **Clean.**
- **`AnnotationActionOverlay.tsx`** -- Deleted. Zero references remain. **Clean.**

### New files (items 3-5)
- **`StrokeOverlayCanvas.tsx`** (#3) -- Clean extraction of the rendering portion of `StrokeOverlay`. Props are well-typed, rendering logic is identical to what was inline before. Handles zero-dimension guard correctly. No state, no side effects, pure rendering. **Clean.**
- **`maskExport.ts`** (#4) -- Clean extraction of the mask export logic. Properly uses try/catch/finally for DOM cleanup. Error handling delegates to `normalizeAndPresentError`. The `finally` block ensures the offscreen container is removed even on error (improvement over the old code which removed in both branches separately). **Clean.**
- **`maskExport.test.ts`** (#5) -- Exists but minimal (see C1 above).

### Modified files

- **`MediaGallery/types.ts`** (#8) -- `onDelete` type changed from `(id: string) => void` to `LightboxDeleteHandler` which is `(id: string) => void | Promise<void>`. This is a backward-compatible widening. **Clean.**

- **`MediaGallery/hooks/index.ts`** (#9) -- Removed `useMobileInteractions` and `useMediaGalleryHandlers` from barrel exports. Both are still used internally via `useMediaGalleryInteractionController` which imports them directly. The module coverage tests import them directly too. **Clean.**

- **`MediaGallery/hooks/useMediaGalleryActions.ts`** (#10) -- `onDelete` type updated to `LightboxDeleteHandler`. Consistent change. **Clean.**

- **`MediaGallery/hooks/useMobileInteractions.ts`** (#11) -- `NodeJS.Timeout` changed to `ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>`. This is more portable (works in both Node and browser environments). **Clean.**

- **`MediaGallery/services/downloadService.ts`** (#12) -- Added `toDownloadServiceError` helper, wrapped `fetch()` and `response.blob()` calls in try/catch. Previously, network failures from `fetch()` would throw raw `TypeError` or `DOMException`; now they're wrapped as `DownloadServiceError` with proper codes. The `DownloadedMediaResult` interface was de-exported (made internal). **Clean.**

- **`MediaGalleryItem.tsx`** (#13) -- `useProject` replaced with `useProjectSelectionContext`. Only uses `selectedProjectId`, so the narrower context is correct. **Clean.**

- **`MediaGalleryItem.test.tsx`** (#14) -- Mock updated to match new import. **Clean.**

- **`ImageLightbox.tsx`** (#15) -- Large refactor extracting builder functions (`buildImageSharedCoreSlice`, `buildImageSharedNavigationSlice`, etc.) out of the inline object literal in `buildImageSharedLightboxInput`. This is a readability improvement -- same data, just named functions instead of one giant object. The `handleDelete` async wrapper addition is consistent with the type change. **Clean.**

- **`MediaLightbox.tsx`** (#16) -- `onDelete` type updated to `LightboxActionHandlers['onDelete']`. Single line change, consistent. **Clean.**

- **`ButtonGroups.tsx`** (#17) -- `onDelete` and `handleDelete` types updated. `void handleDownload()` and `void handleDelete()` added to properly handle the returned promises from click handlers. **Clean.**

- **`WorkflowControls.tsx`** (#19) -- Type updates for `onDelete`/`handleDelete`. **Clean.**

- **`WorkflowControlsBar.tsx`** (#20) -- Type update for `onDelete`. **Clean.**

- **`submitSegmentTask.ts`** (#21) -- `joinPromptParts` replaces manual array-filter-join for prompt affixes; `saveSettings` return type tightened; settings save failure now aborts task creation (see C4). **Clean with noted behavior change.**

- **`useJoinClips.ts`** (#22) -- Storage key now project/user-scoped (see C5). **Clean with noted migration consideration.**

- **`useLightboxShellInteractionHandlers.ts`** (#23) -- Magic number `100000` replaced with `UI_Z_LAYERS.LIGHTBOX_MODAL`. **Clean.**

- **`useLightboxVariantBadges.ts`** (#24) -- Now uses `useVariantBadges` shared hook for badge data when loaded, falling back to local `variants` list when loading. This makes the lightbox badge source consistent with gallery/timeline badges. **Clean.**

- **`useSharedLightboxState.ts`** (#25) -- Major but mechanical refactor: sub-interfaces exported, input types narrowed for each sub-facade (`SharedVariantsInput`, `SharedNavigationInput`, etc.), `handleDelete` made async, all consistent with the `LightboxDeleteHandler` type change. The narrowed input types are a good improvement -- they prevent sub-facades from accidentally depending on unrelated state slices. **Clean.**

- **`useVariantSelection.ts`** (#26) -- Added `viewedGenerationId` prop for more precise optimistic badge updates. Falls back to `getGenerationId(media)` when absent. **Clean.**

- **`useVideoLightboxActions.ts`** (#27) -- `handleDelete` made async with error handling. **Clean.**

- **`useVideoLightboxRenderModel.tsx`** (#28) -- Three large object literals (`lightboxVariants`, `lightboxStateValue`, `workflowBar`, `workflowControls`, `layoutProps`) wrapped in `useMemo` with explicit dependency arrays. This is a significant performance improvement -- these were being recreated on every render before. The dependency arrays look complete and correct upon inspection. **Clean.**

- **`useVideoRegenerateMode.ts`** (#29) -- `extractSegmentImages` import moved from deleted `galleryUtils` to `travelBetweenImages/segmentImages`. `updateToolSettingsSupabase` call updated from `(params, undefined, 'immediate')` to `(params, 'immediate')` -- this is correct because the function has an overload that accepts `(params, mode)` directly. **Clean.**

- **`MediaLightbox/types.ts`** (#30) -- Added `LightboxMaybePromise<T>` utility type and `LightboxDeleteHandler`. Clean type definitions. **Clean.**

- **`MediaLightbox.tsx` barrel** (#31) -- Removed `MediaLightboxFromAdapter` and `toMediaLightboxProps` re-exports. No remaining consumers. **Clean.**

- **`StrokeOverlay.tsx`** (#18) -- Rendering logic extracted to `StrokeOverlayCanvas`, mask export logic extracted to `maskExport.ts`. The `stageRef` was removed (it was only used in the inline code that's now extracted). The `useImperativeHandle` now delegates to the extracted `exportStrokeMask` function. `StrokeOverlayCanvas` handles the zero-dimension guard internally so the parent no longer needs the early return. **Clean.**

- **`MediaGalleryLightbox.tsx`** (#6) -- Adapter pattern replaced with direct `MediaLightbox` props. `LightboxSessionModel` introduced as a typed session object. React Query cache subscription for starred state removed (see C2). **Clean with noted behavior consideration.**

- **`MediaGallery/index.tsx`** (#7) -- Session model assembled inline with full `useMemo` + explicit dependency array. `useProject` split into `useProjectSelectionContext`/`useProjectCrudContext`. Re-export of `GeneratedImageWithMetadata` removed (no external consumers). **Clean.**

### Test changes (items 32-35)
- **`MediaGalleryLightbox.test.tsx`** (#32) -- Properly tests metadata stripping, starred resolution from filtered vs active, and fallback behavior. Well-structured with render helper. **Clean.**
- **`useLightboxVariantBadges.test.ts`** (#33) -- Tests shared badge data source, loading fallback, and mark-all-viewed behavior. **Clean.**
- **`useVariantSelection.test.ts`** (#34) -- Tests `viewedGenerationId` passthrough, fallback to media ID, and deduplication of mark-viewed calls. **Clean.**
- **`submitSegmentTask.test.ts`** (#35) -- Tests the new abort-on-save-failure behavior and success path. **Clean.**
- **`downloadService.test.ts`** (bonus new file) -- Tests error wrapping for network failures, abort signals, HTTP errors, and success path. **Clean.**

---

## Summary

| Severity | Count | Items |
|----------|-------|-------|
| Problems | 0 confirmed | P1 (showMagicEdit) is intentional cleanup of dead feature; P2 is actually an improvement |
| Concerns | 7 | C1 (thin mask test), C2 (starred timing), C3 (duplicated handleDelete), C4 (settings save throws), C5 (storage key migration), C6 (media in deps), C7 (ok actually) |
| Clean | 28 files | All remaining files pass audit |

**Overall assessment:** This is a sound set of changes. The major themes are:
1. **Adapter removal** -- `MediaLightboxInputAdapter` eliminated in favor of direct props. Cleaner, less indirection.
2. **Async delete support** -- `onDelete` type widened to accept `Promise<void>`, with consistent error handling wrappers.
3. **StrokeOverlay extraction** -- Rendering and mask export logic cleanly extracted to separate modules.
4. **Performance memoization** -- Several large object literals in `useVideoLightboxRenderModel` now properly `useMemo`'d.
5. **Type narrowing** -- Sub-facade input types narrowed, `saveSettings` return type tightened, `isDeleting` properly resolved.
6. **Import modernization** -- `useProject` split, `NodeJS.Timeout` replaced, magic numbers extracted to constants.

No breaking regressions found. The behavioral changes (showMagicEdit disabled, saveSettings abort, scoped storage keys) are all improvements over their previous state.

```
     2→import { getSupabaseClient } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     3→
     4→export async function setSessionFromTokens(accessToken: string, refreshToken: string) {
     5→  return getSupabaseClient().auth.setSession({
     6→    access_token: accessToken,
     7→    refresh_token: refreshToken,
     8→  });
     9→}
    10→
    11→export async function fetchCurrentSession() {
    12→  return getSupabaseClient().auth.getSession();
    13→}
    14→
    15→export function subscribeToAuthStateChanges(
    16→  handler: (event: string, session: Session | null) => void,
    17→): () => void {
    18→  const { data: listener } = getSupabaseClient().auth.onAuthStateChange(handler);
    19→  return () => listener.subscription.unsubscribe();
    20→}
    21→
    22→export async function rpcCreateReferralFromSession(sessionId: string, fingerprint: string) {
    23→  return getSupabaseClient().rpc('create_referral_from_session', {
    24→    p_session_id: sessionId,
    25→    p_fingerprint: fingerprint,
    26→  });
    27→}
    28→
```

```
    83→      queryClient.setQueryData(
    84→        shotQueryKey,
    85→        (previousShotData as GenerationRow[]).map((generation) => (
    86→          generation.id === input.generationId ? { ...generation, starred: input.starred } : generation
    87→        )),
    88→      );
    89→    }
    90→  }
    91→
    92→  return {
    93→    previousGenerationsQueries,
    94→    previousShotsQueries,
    95→    previousAllShotGenerationsQueries,
    96→  };
    97→}
    98→
    99→export function rollbackOptimisticGenerationStarUpdate(
   100→  queryClient: QueryClient,
   101→  context?: GenerationStarOptimisticContext,
   102→): void {
   103→  if (!context) {
   104→    return;
   105→  }
   106→
   107→  context.previousGenerationsQueries.forEach((data, key) => {
   108→    queryClient.setQueryData(key, data);
   109→  });
   110→  context.previousShotsQueries.forEach((data, key) => {
   111→    queryClient.setQueryData(key, data);
   112→  });
   113→  context.previousAllShotGenerationsQueries.forEach((data, key) => {
   114→    queryClient.setQueryData(key, data);
   115→  });
   116→}
   117→
```

```
import { describe, expect, it, vi, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { TASK_STATUS } from '@/types/tasks';
import {
  PROCESSING_REFETCH_POLICY,
  shouldForceProcessingRefetch,
} from '@/shared/hooks/tasks/taskFetchPolicy';

describe('shouldForceProcessingRefetch', () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  it('allows stale-empty processing refetches while attempts remain', () => {
    vi.spyOn(Date, 'now').mockReturnValue(100_000);
    Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', {
      configurable: true,
      value: false,
    });

    const shouldRefetch = shouldForceProcessingRefetch(
      [TASK_STATUS.QUEUED, TASK_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS],
      {
        data: { tasks: [] },
        isFetching: false,
        status: 'success',
        dataUpdatedAt: 60_000,
      },
      PROCESSING_REFETCH_POLICY.foregroundMinBackoffMs + 1,
      0,
    );

    expect(shouldRefetch).toBe(true);
  });

  it('stops refetching once stale-empty attempt budget is exhausted', () => {
    vi.spyOn(Date, 'now').mockReturnValue(100_000);
    Object.defineProperty(document, 'hidden', {
      configurable: true,
      value: false,
    });

    const shouldRefetch = shouldForceProcessingRefetch(
      [TASK_STATUS.QUEUED, TASK_STATUS.IN_PROGRESS],
      {
        data: { tasks: [] },
        isFetching: false,
        status: 'success',
        dataUpdatedAt: 60_000,
      },
      PROCESSING_REFETCH_POLICY.foregroundMinBackoffMs + 1,
      PROCESSING_REFETCH_POLICY.maxConsecutiveStaleEmptyRefetches,
    );

    expect(shouldRefetch).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```
   useEffect(() => {

+    const shouldResetAttempts = !hasStaleEmptyData || !isProcessingStatusFilter(status) || query.status !== 'success';
+    if (shouldResetAttempts) {

       lastRefetchRef.current = Date.now();
```

```
+const DEFAULT_SERIALIZED_TIMELINE_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
+
+interface TimelineSerializedWriteOptions {
+  timeoutMs?: number;
+  onTimeout?: (meta: {
+    shotId: string;
+    operation: string;
+    timeoutMs: number;
+    pendingMs: number;
+    requestId: number;
+  }) => void;
+}
+
+function runSerializedTaskWithTimeout<T>(
+  operation: string,
+  task: () => Promise<T>,
+  timeoutMs: number,
+  onTimeout: () => void,
+): Promise<T> {
+  if (timeoutMs <= 0) {
+    return task();
+  }
+
+  return new Promise<T>((resolve, reject) => {
+    let settled = false;
+    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
+      if (settled) {
+        return;
+      }
+      settled = true;
+      onTimeout();
+      reject(new TimelineWriteTimeoutError(operation, timeoutMs));
+    }, timeoutMs);
+
+    Promise.resolve()
+      .then(task)
+      .then(
+        (result) => {
+          if (settled) {
+            return;
+          }
+          settled = true;
+          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
+          resolve(result);
+        },
+        (error) => {
+          if (settled) {
+            return;
+          }
+          settled = true;
+          clearTimeout(timeoutId);
+          reject(error);
+        },
+      );
+  });
+}

  * Returns true if any serialized timeline write is active or queued for the

 ): Promise<T> {
```

```
+type CompletionFollowUpStep =
+  | 'validation'

+  | 'cost_calculation'
+  | 'follow_up_persistence';
+
+interface CompletionFollowUpIssue {
+  step: CompletionFollowUpStep;
+  code: string;

+}

 function defaultErrorCode(status: number): string {
   if (status === 401) return 'authentication_failed';
   if (status === 403) return 'forbidden';
@@ -81,6 +94,47 @@ function completeTaskErrorResponse(

+function asObjectRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
+  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
+    return {};
+  }
+  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
+}
+
+async function persistCompletionFollowUpIssues(
+  supabase: SupabaseClient,
+  taskId: string,
+  existingResultData: unknown,
+  issues: CompletionFollowUpIssue[],
+): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: unknown }> {
+  if (issues.length === 0) {
+    return { ok: true };
+  }
+
+  const baseResultData = asObjectRecord(existingResultData);
+  const completionFollowUp = {
+    status: 'degraded',
+    recorded_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+    issues,
+  };
+
+  const { error } = await supabase
+    .from('tasks')
+    .update({
+      result_data: {
+        ...baseResultData,
+        completion_follow_up: completionFollowUp,
+      },
+    })
+    .eq('id', taskId);
+
+  if (error) {
+    return { ok: false, error };
+  }
+
+  return { ok: true };
+}
```

# Dirty File Review: `chore/desloppify-queue-2026-03-02`

## FLAGGED CONCERNS

**CONCERN 1 -- ShotListView missing null guard on star toggle (minor)**
File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/pages/ShotListView.tsx` line 209. The `handleToggleStar` callback passes `selectedProjectId` directly to `toggleStarMutation.mutate()` without a null guard. Other tool pages (image-generation, join-clips, character-animate) all guard with `if (!projectId) return`. However, in ShotListView, `selectedProjectId` is typed as `string` (non-nullable) in the component props (line 31), so this is **safe at runtime** but inconsistent with the pattern used elsewhere. Low risk.

**CONCERN 2 -- Lightbox containers are pure pass-through wrappers (design question)**
Files: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/containers/ImageLightboxContainer.tsx` and `VideoLightboxContainer.tsx`. These are 1:1 prop-forwarding wrappers with zero logic. They add an indirection layer without any current benefit. If the intent is to add container-level logic later, fine. If not, these could be direct imports. Not a bug, but worth noting as potential unnecessary abstraction.

**CONCERN 3 -- `check-legacy-supabase-usage.mjs` now requires `tsx` runtime**
File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/package.json`. The script was changed from `node` to `tsx` because it now imports TypeScript from `legacySupabasePolicy.ts`. This means `tsx` must be a dev dependency and available in CI. If it's already in `devDependencies`, no issue. If not, the script will fail.

---

## Supabase Repositories (new files)

### 1. `src/integrations/supabase/repositories/homeAuthRepository.ts`
**What changed:** New file. Extracts 4 Supabase auth operations (setSession, getSession, onAuthStateChange, createReferralFromSession) into pure repository functions.
**Verdict:** Sound. Clean extraction, thin wrappers, no logic beyond delegation. Matches the repository pattern used by other files in this directory.

### 2. `src/integrations/supabase/repositories/projectSetupRepository.ts`
**What changed:** New file. Extracts 5 Supabase DB/RPC operations (copy_onboarding_template, delete project, insert shot, fetch user, create_user_record_if_not_exists) into repository functions.
**Verdict:** Sound. Same pattern as homeAuthRepository. Proper typing with `Json` import.

### 3. `src/integrations/supabase/legacy/legacySupabaseAllowlist.json`
**What changed:** Was deleted in the previous commit; now exists as an untracked file (9KB). Appears to be a regenerated desloppify tool artifact.
**Verdict:** Neutral. Not referenced by any production code. The legacySupabasePolicy.ts does not import it. This is a tool artifact.

---

## Generation Hooks

### 4. `src/domains/generation/hooks/useDeleteGenerationAction.ts`
**What changed:** Hook now takes `{ projectId }` as an explicit parameter instead of pulling it from `useProjectSelectionContext()`. Error handling upgraded from silent `catch {}` to `normalizeAndPresentError` with toast.
**Verdict:** Sound. Good decoupling from context. The error presentation adds user feedback for failed deletes while keeping the dialog open for retry.

### 5. `src/domains/generation/hooks/useDeleteGenerationWithConfirm.ts`
**What changed:** Passthrough of the new `{ projectId }` option to `useDeleteGenerationAction`.
**Verdict:** Sound. Mechanical propagation.

### 6. `src/domains/generation/hooks/useGenerationMutations.ts`
**What changed:** The `useToggleGenerationStar` mutation's `onMutate`/`onError` optimistic update logic (~65 lines) extracted to `generationStarCacheCoordinator.ts`. Import type changed from `@/types/shots` to `@/domains/generation/types`. Removed unused `Shot` import and `queryKeys` import.
**Verdict:** Sound. The extraction is 1:1 faithful. The inlined optimistic update logic was complex and now lives in a testable, standalone module. The coordinator is tested (see #19/#20).

### 7. `src/domains/generation/hooks/__tests__/useDeleteGenerationAction.test.ts`
**What changed:** Tests updated to pass `{ projectId }` instead of mocking `useProjectSelectionContext`. New test for null project scope (verifies mutateAsync is not called). New assertion that `normalizeAndPresentError` is called on failure.
**Verdict:** Sound. Good coverage of the new null-project guard.

### 8. `src/domains/generation/hooks/__tests__/useDeleteGenerationWithConfirm.test.ts`
**What changed:** Tests updated to pass `{ projectId }` and verify it's forwarded to the action hook.
**Verdict:** Sound.

---

## MediaGallery / Lightbox

### 9. `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/components/MediaGalleryLightbox.tsx`
**What changed:** The session model was flattened. Previously used 8 nested sub-interfaces (`core`, `navigation`, `actions`, `shotWorkflow`, `ui`, `optimistic`, `taskModal`, `taskData`, `integration`); now uses a single flat `MediaGalleryLightboxSession` interface. The component destructures directly from `session` instead of nested groups.
**Verdict:** Sound. The nested grouping added indirection without benefit -- all properties are consumed at the same level. The flat interface is simpler and the component body is shorter. `onServerPageChange` was removed from the session type (it was never consumed inside the lightbox itself; navigation hooks wrap it into `onNext`/`onPrevious`).

### 10. `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/components/__tests__/MediaGalleryLightbox.test.tsx`
**What changed:** Test session object updated to match the new flat structure.
**Verdict:** Sound. Mechanical update.

### 11. `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.tsx`
**What changed:** (a) Removed `useToggleGenerationStar` import/usage -- star toggle now comes from the parent via `onToggleStar` prop. (b) `showStar` guarded with `typeof onToggleStar === 'function'` to prevent showing a non-functional button. (c) Removed `toggleStarMutation` from `itemActions`. (d) Lightbox session construction flattened to match new `MediaGalleryLightboxSession`.
**Verdict:** Sound. The star toggle responsibility is correctly lifted to the page/tool level, making `MediaGallery` a pure presentational component for this concern. The defensive guard on `showStar` is a good addition.

### 12. `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem.tsx`
**What changed:** Removed `toggleStarMutation` from destructured `actions` and from `ActionButtons` props.
**Verdict:** Sound. Follows the pattern of removing the mutation from the shared component.

### 13. `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem/components/ActionButtons.tsx`
**What changed:** Removed `projectId` and `toggleStarMutation` props. Star toggle logic simplified: if `onToggleStar` is not provided, reverts local state and returns. If provided, calls it directly. Removed the fallback path that called `toggleStarMutation.mutate()` directly.
**Verdict:** Sound. This eliminates the dual-path toggle logic (callback vs. direct mutation). The component is now purely callback-driven, which is cleaner.

### 14. `src/shared/components/MediaGalleryItem/types.ts`
**What changed:** Removed `toggleStarMutation` from `ItemActions` interface.
**Verdict:** Sound.

### 15. `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/MediaLightbox.tsx`
**What changed:** Replaced direct `ImageLightbox`/`VideoLightbox` rendering with `ImageLightboxContainer`/`VideoLightboxContainer`. Extracted shared props into a `sharedContainerProps` memoized object. Created a `renderVideoLightbox` helper to avoid duplicating the VideoLightbox JSX for the two code paths (segmentSlotMode and isVideoAny).
**Verdict:** Sound. The shared container props are correctly memoized. The `renderVideoLightbox` helper eliminates the duplicated VideoLightbox JSX. Note concern #2 above about whether the container abstraction layer adds value.

### 16. `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/WorkflowControls.tsx`
**What changed:** The component body was replaced with a delegation to `WorkflowControlsBar`. All UI rendering (shot selector, add-to-shot buttons, apply settings) was removed from this file. It now just passes props through. The `useShotAssociationControls` hook import was removed.
**Verdict:** Sound. This is a consolidation -- `WorkflowControlsBar` already existed as a parallel implementation, and now `WorkflowControls` delegates to it instead of maintaining duplicate UI code. The `onNavigateToShot` prop was added to the passthrough.

### 17. `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/components/submitSegmentTask.ts`
**What changed:** (a) Added `onNonFatalError` callback for reporting non-fatal side-effect failures (enhance_prompt, metadata_fetch, metadata_update). (b) Replaced hardcoded `'submitSegmentTask'` error context strings with `runtime.errorContext`. (c) Non-fatal errors now pass `showToast: false` to prevent user-facing toasts for background failures. (d) Removed the `as unknown as` type cast by fixing `buildTaskParams` return type upstream. (e) Exported `IndividualTravelSegmentParams` type from the individual travel segment module.
**Verdict:** Sound. The non-fatal error reporting is a good observability improvement. Suppressing toasts for metadata update failures is correct -- these shouldn't disrupt the user. The type cast removal is a genuine type safety improvement.

### 18. `src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/containers/` (new directory)
Contains 3 files:
- `lightboxContainerContracts.ts`: Defines `LightboxSharedContainerProps`, `ImageLightboxContainerProps`, `VideoLightboxContainerProps` interfaces.
- `ImageLightboxContainer.tsx`: Passes shared+image props to `ImageLightbox`.
- `VideoLightboxContainer.tsx`: Passes shared+video props to `VideoLightbox`.

**Verdict:** Sound but thin (see concern #2). These are pure pass-through wrappers. The contract file is useful for typing. The containers themselves add no logic.

### 19. `src/shared/hooks/invalidation/generationStarCacheCoordinator.ts` (new)
**What changed:** New file extracted from `useToggleGenerationStar`. Contains `applyOptimisticGenerationStarUpdate` (cancels queries, snapshots, applies optimistic updates across unified/shots/byShot caches) and `rollbackOptimisticGenerationStarUpdate`. Includes a proper type guard `isUnifiedGenerationListData`.
**Verdict:** Sound. Clean extraction. The type guard is an improvement over the inline `typeof data === 'object' && 'items' in data` check. Handles the undefined `context` case in rollback.

### 20. `src/shared/hooks/invalidation/__tests__/generationStarCacheCoordinator.test.ts` (new)
**What changed:** Tests for the apply and rollback functions using real `QueryClient` instances.
**Verdict:** Sound. Tests both the happy path (star updates across all three cache types) and the rollback path.

---

## Tasks / Timeline

### 21. `src/shared/hooks/tasks/taskFetchPolicy.ts`
**What changed:** (a) Added `PROCESSING_REFETCH_POLICY` exported constant with named thresholds. (b) `shouldForceProcessingRefetch` now accepts a `consecutiveStaleEmptyRefetches` parameter and checks it against `maxConsecutiveStaleEmptyRefetches` (3). (c) Magic numbers replaced with named constants.
**Verdict:** Sound. This adds a circuit breaker: after 3 consecutive stale-empty refetches, it stops polling. Prevents infinite refetching when the server consistently returns empty results. The default parameter value (0) maintains backward compatibility.

### 22. `src/shared/hooks/tasks/taskFetchPolicy.test.ts` (new)
**What changed:** Tests that refetches are allowed while attempts remain and stopped when budget is exhausted.
**Verdict:** Sound. Covers the core circuit-breaker logic.

### 23. `src/shared/hooks/tasks/useProcessingRefetchGuard.ts`
**What changed:** Adds `staleEmptyRefetchCountRef` and `previousStatusKeyRef` refs. Resets the count when status filter changes or when the guard condition no longer applies. Passes the count to `shouldForceProcessingRefetch`. Increments on each refetch. Early-returns when terminal guard state is reached.
**Verdict:** Sound. The reset logic is correct: changing the status filter resets the counter (different query context), and getting non-empty data resets it. The terminal state early return prevents unnecessary work.

### 24. `src/shared/lib/__tests__/timelineWriteQueue.test.ts`
**What changed:** New test: verifies that a timed-out serialized write releases the queue so subsequent writes can proceed. Uses `vi.useFakeTimers()` and `vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync`.
**Verdict:** Sound. Good test for the timeout/recovery behavior.

### 25. `src/shared/lib/timelineWriteQueue.ts`
**What changed:** Added `runSerializedTaskWithTimeout` function that wraps a task with a configurable timeout (default 30s). The `runSerializedTimelineWrite` function now accepts `options?: { timeoutMs, onTimeout }` and uses this wrapper. A custom `TimelineWriteTimeoutError` is thrown on timeout. The `settled` flag prevents double-resolve/reject races.
**Verdict:** Sound. The timeout mechanism correctly handles the race between task completion and timeout. The `settled` flag is the right pattern. The `releaseTail()` in the `finally` block (unchanged) ensures the queue is always released, even on timeout. The `onTimeout` callback provides observability.

### 26. `src/shared/lib/tasks/individualTravelSegment.ts`
**What changed:** `IndividualTravelSegmentParams` interface exported (was previously private).
**Verdict:** Sound. Needed to eliminate the `as unknown as` cast in `submitSegmentTask.ts`.

**Verdict:** Sound. Clean extraction, no logic changes.
